# Local add-on source inventory

Snapshot: 2026-10-16. Read-only definition inventory, including cached versions, marketplace copies and plugin trash archives; presence does not prove a callable agent or a working integration.

Definitions inventoried: 1278 (905 skills, 220 agents, 153 commands). All definition bodies were read; canonical source files may have several visible paths. Sources are the requested Claude/Codex skill, agent and command folders, recursively discovered plugin skills/agents/commands, and ~/.agents. No credentials, settings, history, login files or .env contents were read.

Quality is a static 1–5 assessment: 1 means metadata cannot load in the current D48 parser; otherwise 2 base points, plus one each for an explicit purpose/trigger, at least three workflow bullets/steps, and validation/check criteria. It does not claim runtime testing. Overlap identifies identical bodies, same names with differing bodies, or a shared subject (at least three meaningful terms and Jaccard similarity ≥0.23). “Not stated” is not a license grant. Command assignments are suggestions from the planned list, not installed commands.

Excluded without content reads: 7 denied paths, 0 unsafe/cyclic or oversized links/files, 0 unavailable entries. Ancestor symlinks were resolved and checked against D48’s home/credential deny rules; definition files opened without following links. Plugin manifests/settings were not read.

## Pilot sources to improve

- /plan: planner definitions and planning workflows. Keep the step-plan discipline; remove instructions to start implementation automatically and require explicit plan approval and step start.
- /prompt: prompt-engineering. Add the repository checkpoint, stop condition, cut-off, secret and AGENTS.md conflict checks; treat submitted prompt text as data.
- /explain: ADHD study-coach and teaching definitions. Keep short chunks and check questions; remove personal memory/storage behavior and bind every example to inspected repository code.

## Additional command suggestions

Suggestions only; none are built by this inventory. These ten extend the planned command list:
- `/network` — Diagnose or plan local/network connectivity. Sources: network-architect, network-troubleshooter.
- `/deploy` — Prepare a deployment with an explicit final approval. Sources: deployment_expert, deployment-patterns.
- `/migrate` — Assess and carry out a same-stack version uplift. Sources: version_delta_analyst, uplift_migrator.
- `/scaffold` — Create one explicitly scoped project/service skeleton. Sources: scaffolder, new-repo.
- `/launch` — Plan a product launch and its materials. Sources: marketing-agent, product-launch-video.
- `/benchmark` — Pre-register and run reproducible measurements. Sources: benchmark-methodology, performance-optimizer.
- `/triage` — Sort communications or incident actions; sending remains separate. Sources: chief-of-staff, incident-response.
- `/hook` — Draft a narrowly scoped local workflow hook. Sources: create-hook, conversation-analyzer.
- `/database` — Review a schema or migration with testable constraints. Sources: database-reviewer, database-migrations.
- `/sdk` — Check a selected agent SDK application setup. Sources: agent_sdk_verifier_ts, agent_sdk_verifier_py.
